  这位同学澄清说，他的意思是诊所拍照时，病人就要签署或同意某些东西，转移就被涵盖在内了。

  对，我明白你的意思。

  另一位同学说，对于转到欧洲的部分，他们大概可以依靠法律，主张GDPR与新加坡法律大体相当，走这条路。

  对，有可能。你可以说，由于意大利是欧盟成员，那里的处理者会受GDPR约束，而GDPR可以说与PDPA相当。

- [19:46:44–19:49:06] **Discussion: The Onward Transfer to the US**

  How about the US?

  A student says that in that case, the agreement should also ensure that the onward forwarding to the US provider complies as well, so that comparable protection is required for the manual processing in the US.

  Okay, and how would you achieve that, as far as the transfer to the US is concerned?

  The student answers: through contracts.

  Right, so most likely the solution is some sort of contract. And importantly, remember that you have to be a party to the contract, which means you cannot go through the Italian processor. There has to be a direct contract between Plasco and whoever it is in the US, to ensure that there is this legally enforceable obligation.

  A student refers back to an earlier slide, noting that Plasco, as a data intermediary, is not itself subject to the Transfer Limitation Obligation, though it still needs to account for the Protection Obligation.

  Yes. As far as Plasco is concerned, if there's an investigation, Plasco is fine, because it's just a data intermediary of the clinics. It's the clinics that are in trouble. But the clinics know this, so they'll make sure that Plasco complies on their behalf.

  A student asks to confirm: so you would need a contract with the US company, which means the US company has to sign the agreement?

  Basically, the answer is yes. The current example stops at two, but there could be other examples where there are further transfers to other countries, in which case you need to ensure that all the transfers comply with the Transfer Limitation Obligation. But in practice, I think it's fairly unlikely that you'll have a very extended chain of transfers.

  那美国呢？

  一位同学说，这种情况下，协议还应确保转给美国服务商的再转移也合规，要求美国的人工处理也有相当的保护。

  好，那就转到美国这部分而言，你要怎么做到？

  这位同学回答：通过合同。

  对，最可能的解决办法是某种合同。重要的是，记住你必须是合同当事方，这意味着你不能经由意大利处理者来安排。必须由 Plasco 与美国那边的机构直接签订合同，才能确保存在这种具有法律约束力的义务。

  一位同学提到之前的一张幻灯片，指出 Plasco 作为数据中介本身不受转移限制义务约束，但仍需考虑保护义务。

  对。就 Plasco 而言，如果有调查，Plasco 没事，因为它只是诊所的数据中介。有麻烦的是诊所。但诊所知道这一点，所以它们会确保 Plasco 代表它们做到合规。

  一位同学确认：所以你需要和美国公司签合同，也就是说美国公司必须签署这份协议？

  基本上答案是肯定的。目前这个例子只到两层，但也可能有其他例子涉及继续转到其他国家，这时你需要确保所有转移都遵守转移限制义务。不过在实践中，我认为出现非常长的转移链的可能性相当低。

- [19:49:06–19:50:17] **Discussion: Facial Images from the Malaysian and Thai Branches**

  A student points out that the scenario mentions branches in Kuala Lumpur and Bangkok, where the local clinics send their data directly to the local office. So Singapore clinics send to the Singapore office, Malaysian clinics send to the Malaysian office, and that data doesn't even go to Singapore.

  Yes. So, to be clear, there are three sets of facial images. There are those collected by the Singapore office and sent to Italy; that transfer must comply with the Transfer Limitation Obligation. Then there are the images collected in Thailand and Malaysia and sent to the same Italian processor. Because you're transferring out of Malaysia or Thailand, those branch offices must comply with the Malaysian and Thai rules.

  Okay, so that's the Transfer Limitation Obligation.

  一位同学指出，情景中提到在吉隆坡和曼谷设有分公司，当地诊所直接把数据发给当地办公室。所以新加坡诊所发给新加坡办公室，马来西亚诊所发给马来西亚办公室，这些数据根本不经过新加坡。

  对。所以说清楚一点，共有三组面部图像。一组是由新加坡办公室收集并发往意大利的，这次转移必须遵守转移限制义务。另外是在泰国和马来西亚收集、然后发给同一家意大利处理者的图像。由于是从马来西亚或泰国转出，这些分公司必须遵守马来西亚和泰国的规则。

  好，这就是转移限制义务部分。

- [19:50:17–19:51:55] **Discussion: The Employee Records**

  How about the employee records? To what extent must Plasco comply with the PDPA?

  A student says that in this case Plasco would have to comply, because although the employees are not in Singapore, the processing of their data is taking place in Singapore. Plasco would therefore have to abide by all the rules that come with it, such as protecting the data against unauthorised access, not improperly using or disclosing it between systems, and only keeping it for the appropriate period of time.

  So as far as compliance for the employee records is concerned, what Plasco basically needs to do is comply with the usual set of obligations: the consent obligation in relation to the collection of the personal data, and, when storing the personal data, the Protection Obligation, the Retention Limitation Obligation, and so on. What it does not need to be concerned with are the processing activities that take place outside Singapore. So that's how the PDPA would potentially apply to the employee records.

  那员工记录呢？Plasco 在多大程度上必须遵守PDPA？

  一位同学说，在这种情况下 Plasco 必须遵守，因为尽管员工不在新加坡，但其数据的处理发生在新加坡。因此 Plasco 必须遵守随之而来的所有规则，比如保护数据免遭未经授权的访问、不在各系统之间不当使用或披露数据，以及只在适当期限内保留数据。

  所以就员工记录的合规而言，Plasco 基本上需要遵守通常的一套义务：在收集个人数据方面遵守同意义务；存储个人数据时遵守保护义务、保留限制义务等等。它不需要关心的是发生在新加坡境外的处理活动。这就是PDPA可能适用于员工记录的方式。

- [19:52:08–19:53:44] **Q&A: What Must the Clinic–Plasco Contract Say?**

  Any questions so far?

  A student says that, since the case study highlights that the clinics are the ultimate customers of these services, they are trying to see it from the clinic's perspective. In their own contract with Plasco, does the clinic need to go into detail, like, if you're going to send this to Italy, or to the US, or anywhere else, make sure it's covered? Or is it sufficient, from the clinic's perspective, to say: you're going to handle data for us, so ensure that you comply with Singapore's PDPA, full stop? Does it need to go any further?

  As far as the contract between the clinics and Plasco is concerned, I think the answer is that the contract just needs to say: if you're going to transfer data out of Singapore, you need to ensure that you are in compliance with the Transfer Limitation Obligation. It would then be for Plasco to carry out the relevant steps to comply with the Transfer Limitation Obligation.

  The student thanks me and confirms that the clinic-to-Plasco contract therefore doesn't need to be that detailed.

  到目前为止有什么问题吗？

  一位同学说，由于案例强调诊所是这些服务的最终客户，他想从诊所的角度来看。诊所在与 Plasco 的合同中，是否需要写得很细，比如"如果你要把数据发到意大利、美国或其他地方，要确保有保障"？还是从诊所的角度，只要写"你替我们处理数据，请确保遵守新加坡的PDPA"，就此打住，就足够了？还需要更进一步吗？

  就诊所与 Plasco 之间的合同而言，我认为答案是：合同只需写明，如果你要把数据转出新加坡，你必须确保遵守转移限制义务。然后由 Plasco 去采取相关措施来遵守转移限制义务。

  这位同学表示感谢，并确认诊所与 Plasco 之间的合同因此不需要写得那么详细。

- [19:53:44–19:55:06] **Q&A: Employee Data Sent to Singapore from Malaysia and Thailand**

  Another student asks about when the employee information is processed in Singapore, but the company operates in different countries and the employees are foreign nationals, for example Indonesians. Are there any restrictions? Are there rules, such as might apply in Malaysia, governing this?

  Yes. If you are transferring employee data from Malaysia to Singapore, or from Thailand to Singapore, then you need to comply with the Malaysian data protection law rules relating to transfer, and the Thai data protection law rules relating to transfer. But it will not be the Singapore Transfer Limitation Obligation that you need to be concerned about.

  The student says that still, as a company, they could do more and provide more protection.

  Yes. You can't escape the Malaysian and Thai laws, but it won't be Singapore law that governs whatever is happening in that transfer.

  Okay, any other questions?

  另一位同学问：员工信息在新加坡处理，但公司在不同国家经营，员工是外国国民，比如印度尼西亚人。有没有什么限制？比如马来西亚是否有规则规管这种情况？

  有的。如果你把员工数据从马来西亚转到新加坡，或从泰国转到新加坡，那就需要遵守马来西亚数据保护法中关于转移的规定，以及泰国数据保护法中关于转移的规定。但你需要关心的不会是新加坡的转移限制义务。

  这位同学说，即便如此，作为公司，他们还是可以做得更多，提供更多保护。

  对。你逃不开马来西亚和泰国的法律，但规管那次转移的不会是新加坡法律。

  好，还有其他问题吗？

- [19:55:15–19:56:05] **Introducing the Accountability Obligation**

  Alright, so that's transfer limitation and extraterritorial application, both of which basically deal with situations where a foreign entity is involved. The next obligation, and the final one we have to deal with in this course, is the so-called Accountability Obligation.

  So what is the Accountability Obligation? It's a bit of a mixed bag of obligations, but there are basically two things you need to do. Number one, you need to appoint a data protection officer, and number two, you need to develop and implement data protection policies and practices. The second one overlaps with the Protection Obligation, which also requires you to have policies. But as you'll see, this one is a bit broader than what the Protection Obligation requires.

  好，以上就是转移限制和域外适用，两者基本上都处理涉及外国实体的情况。下一项义务，也是本课程要讲的最后一项，是所谓的问责义务（Accountability Obligation）。

  那么什么是问责义务？它有点像一个义务的大杂烩，但基本上你需要做两件事。第一，你需要任命一名数据保护官（data protection officer，DPO）；第二，你需要制定并实施数据保护政策和做法。第二点与保护义务有重叠，保护义务也要求你有政策。但你会看到，这一项比保护义务的要求要宽一些。

- [19:56:24–19:58:08] **Appointing a DPO and the DPO's Role**

  So let's begin with the DPO. This rule is pretty simple. It requires any organisation to designate one or more individuals to be responsible for ensuring that the organisation complies with the PDPA, and this person is often referred to as the data protection officer.

  What the DPO does is not really defined in the PDPA, but generally what the DPO is responsible for is what you can see on the slide here: liaising with senior management, developing policies, maintaining an inventory of the personal data, training the staff, conducting the relevant assessments, and importantly, serving as the organisation's point of contact to deal with complaints. So that's the usual job scope of the DPO.

  In addition, organisations must make available the business contact information of at least one of their DPOs. Often there is just one. How do you do this? The conventional way is to just include the DPO's email address in the organisation's external-facing privacy policy. For example, if you look at NUS, this is the NUS data protection policy, and here you can see the data protection policy specifies the email address and contact details of the NUS DPO. That's how this is normally done.

  我们先从DPO讲起。这条规则很简单。它要求任何机构指定一名或多名个人，负责确保机构遵守PDPA，这个人通常被称为数据保护官。

  PDPA并没有真正界定DPO的职责，但一般来说DPO负责的是幻灯片上列出的这些：与高级管理层联络、制定政策、维护个人数据清单、培训员工、进行相关评估，以及很重要的一点，担任机构处理投诉的联络人。这就是DPO通常的工作范围。

  此外，机构必须公开至少一名DPO的业务联系信息，通常也只有一名。怎么做？惯常做法是把DPO的电邮地址写进机构对外的隐私政策。比如看NUS，这是NUS的数据保护政策，这里可以看到政策中列明了NUS DPO的电邮地址和联系方式。通常就是这样做的。

- [19:58:08–20:00:41] **Who Should Be Appointed as DPO; Case: M Stars Movers**

  The next question that could arise is: who do you actually appoint as your DPO? The PDPA also doesn't say anything about this, but as we know, there's a general overarching standard, which is reasonableness. Organisations have to act reasonably when trying to comply with the Accountability Obligation, and that applies to how you choose your DPO.

  In terms of guidelines, what they say is that when choosing a DPO, you need someone who actually knows the area, and who is empowered, in the sense of having the authority to do what is needed to make sure the organisation is compliant. But the DPO need not be an employee, so you are allowed to outsource your DPO role to some third party, and there are a few companies that provide this kind of service.

  The case of M Stars Movers recommends something which may or may not be realistic in practice, but this is what it recommends: that the DPO should be appointed from the ranks of senior management, or at least have a direct line of communication to the management of the company. The reason is that the DPO will often need to make certain decisions that affect the company as a whole. So you need someone who either has the authority to do those things, or has a way to communicate with the leaders who have the authority to make them.

  So those are the general guidelines. Of course, these guidelines don't apply across the board. For example, if you have a really small outfit of, I don't know, four people, these guidelines generally don't matter that much. In that case, you just pick the person best placed to serve as the DPO.

  接下来可能出现的问题是：你实际上要任命谁当DPO？PDPA对此也没有规定，但我们知道有一个总体的一般标准，就是合理性。机构在设法遵守问责义务时必须合理行事，这也适用于你如何选择DPO。

  就指南而言，它们说的是，选择DPO时，你需要一个真正懂这个领域的人，而且他要有权力，也就是有权限去做必要的事情，确保机构合规。但DPO不一定是雇员，所以你可以把DPO职能外包给第三方，也有几家公司提供这类服务。

  M Stars Movers 案提出了一个建议，在实践中未必现实，但它是这样建议的：DPO应从高级管理层中任命，或至少有一条直达公司管理层的沟通渠道。原因是DPO常常需要作出影响整个公司的某些决定。所以你需要一个本身有权做这些事，或者能够与有权作决定的领导沟通的人。

  这些是一般性的指南。当然，这些指南并非处处适用。比如，如果你是一个很小的团队，就四个人左右，这些指南一般就没那么重要了。这种情况下，你只要挑最合适的人担任DPO就行。

- [20:00:41–20:01:42] **Developing and Implementing Data Protection Policies and Practices**

  So that's appointing a DPO, which is pretty simple. The other thing, which is more complicated, is that you are required to develop and implement data protection policies and practices. Specifically, four things.

  Number one, develop policies and practices to enable the organisation to comply with the PDPA. Number two, develop a complaint process, meaning how you are going to deal with situations where individuals complain to you about something you've done wrong. Number three, provide staff training and communicate information about your policies and practices. And number four, tell the public, upon request, what policies and practices you have, as well as your complaint process.

  So that's the idea behind it: you can demonstrate how you are going to comply with the PDPA.

  以上是任命DPO，相当简单。另一件更复杂的事，是你需要制定并实施数据保护政策和做法。具体来说有四件事。

  第一，制定使机构能够遵守PDPA的政策和做法。第二，建立投诉处理流程，也就是当个人就你做错的事向你投诉时，你会如何处理。第三，提供员工培训，并传达关于你的政策和做法的信息。第四，应公众要求，告知你有哪些政策和做法，以及你的投诉处理流程。

  这背后的思路是：你能够证明你将如何遵守PDPA。

- [20:01:42–20:04:08] **Policies Must Be in Writing; Case: Habitat for Humanity**

  Some of the cases have discussed this particular part of the Accountability Obligation. One standard rule that applies is that your policies and practices should be documented in writing.

  The case illustrating this point is the Habitat for Humanity case. Here you have a charity that seeks to provide housing to the poor, and it organises a number of CIPs, or community involvement programmes, like mass clean-ups of housing, and invites volunteers to take part. After each event, the organisation sends out letters to thank and acknowledge the volunteers.

  So how do they send out the letters? Normally, the manager prepares the letters in a PDF document, and this is sent to the admin staff, who split the document and send the letters out to individual volunteers. Here, the admin staff member, who I think was a new person, simply attached the whole PDF document containing all the letters into one email and sent it to all the volunteers. Of course, that's a wrongful disclosure of personal data.

  The PDPC investigated the organisation, and one of the breaches was of the Accountability Obligation, because the organisation didn't have any documented policy dealing with the sending of CIP letters. Although the organisation said it had verbally instructed the admin staff on how to send out the CIP letters, the PDPC said this was not enough. The expectation is that the policy should be in writing, so that the staff member who needs to perform the activity has something to refer to. It also ensures that whatever policy the organisation has created is passed down properly.

  有些案例讨论过问责义务的这一部分。一条标准规则是，你的政策和做法应以书面形式记录。

  说明这一点的是 Habitat for Humanity 案。这是一家致力于为穷人提供住房的慈善机构，它组织多项CIP，即社区参与计划（community involvement programmes），比如大规模清洁房屋，并邀请志愿者参与。每次活动后，机构会寄出信件感谢并表彰志愿者。

  信是怎么寄出的？通常由经理把信件制作成一份PDF文件，发给行政人员，由行政人员拆分文件，再分别发给个别志愿者。在本案中，这名行政人员——我记得是个新人——直接把包含所有信件的整份PDF附在一封邮件里，发给了所有志愿者。这当然是对个人数据的不当披露。

  PDPC调查了该机构，其中一项违规是违反问责义务，因为该机构没有任何关于寄发CIP信件的书面政策。尽管机构表示曾口头指示行政人员如何寄发CIP信件，PDPC认为这还不够。期望是政策应以书面形式存在，让需要执行该活动的员工有东西可以参照。这也能确保机构制定的政策被正确地传达下去。

- [20:04:08–20:06:45] **External Policies Are Not Enough; Case: Xbot**

  Another important thing that has developed from the cases is that the PDPC has clarified that you cannot just rely on your external-facing data protection policy. One of the cases that talks about this is the Xbot case.

  Here, the organisation is a very small outfit that developed the Strata.sg mobile app and website. The purpose of Strata.sg is to provide access to a database of residential property transactions. So you could find out, for example, how much property in a particular location sold for over the past five years. That's what the database is for.

  In this case, the organisation had the usual external-facing privacy policy, but notably, did it have any internal policies? No. It just had this one PDF document for external customers. The PDPC got a complaint because someone was not happy that their transaction data was disclosed on this database. But the PDPC said this was not a breach of the Consent Obligation, because the data was publicly available.

  The PDPC nevertheless said the organisation had breached the Accountability Obligation. The reason is that although it had this external data protection policy, like the one I showed you, which you can find on most websites, an external-facing policy is meant to inform individuals about how their personal data will be handled. What you really need is an internal policy that is meant to guide your staff, your employees, on their activities and what they need to do to comply with the PDPA.

  So why is that? Here the PDPC said that even though this organisation was a two-person organisation, one director and one employee, the organisation still needed to have internal policies governing what that one employee was doing. So it's very strict.

  从案例中发展出的另一个要点是，PDPC已经明确表示，你不能仅仅依靠对外的数据保护政策。讨论这一点的案例之一是 Xbot 案。

  该机构是一个很小的团队，开发了 Strata.sg 手机应用和网站。Strata.sg 的用途是提供住宅物业交易数据库的访问。比如你可以查到某个地点的房产在过去五年卖了多少钱。这个数据库就是干这个的。

  在本案中，该机构有常见的对外隐私政策，但值得注意的是，它有没有任何内部政策？没有。它只有这一份给外部客户看的PDF文件。PDPC收到投诉，因为有人不满自己的交易数据在这个数据库上被披露。但PDPC表示这并不违反同意义务，因为这些数据是公开可得的。

  尽管如此，PDPC仍认定该机构违反了问责义务。理由是，虽然它有这份对外的数据保护政策——就像我给你们看的那种，大多数网站上都能找到——但对外政策的作用是告知个人他们的个人数据将如何被处理。你真正需要的是内部政策，用来指导你的员工开展活动，告诉他们需要做什么才能遵守PDPA。

  为什么要这样？在本案中，PDPC表示，即使该机构只有两个人，一名董事和一名雇员，它仍然需要有内部政策来规管那一名雇员的工作。所以要求非常严格。

- [20:06:58–20:08:11] **Policies Must Be Specific to the Organisation's Activities; Case: Spize Concepts**

  Another thing to bear in mind is that whenever you develop these policies and practices, they don't need to be very complicated, but they do need to be specific to the activities that you are doing.

  For example, you have this case where the organisation, Spize, had a website and engaged a vendor called Novadine to develop and host it. But because the organisation was ignorant about how Novadine's system worked, it enabled a certain feature that basically allowed members of the public to view other people's order data.

  Here, the PDPC said again there was a breach of the Accountability Obligation, because the organisation needed to develop internal data protection policies specifically addressing Novadine's web hosting services, to instruct the staff on how to use Novadine's web hosting.

  另一点要记住的是，每当你制定这些政策和做法时，它们不需要很复杂，但确实需要针对你所开展的具体活动。

  比如有这样一个案例：机构 Spize 有一个网站，并聘请了一家名叫 Novadine 的供应商来开发和托管网站。但由于该机构不了解 Novadine 的系统如何运作，它启用了某项功能，基本上让公众能够查看其他人的订单数据。

  在本案中，PDPC再次认定违反了问责义务，因为该机构需要制定专门针对 Novadine 网站托管服务的内部数据保护政策，指导员工如何使用 Novadine 的网站托管服务。

- [20:08:11–20:10:29] **Exercise: Drafting an External-Facing Notice for Resupro**

  Okay, so those are the general principles regarding the Accountability Obligation. What we can do now is look at this example. I have two questions here: one is external-facing, and one is internal-facing.

  So here you are Levin, the co-founder of a startup, Resupro. You have five employees, and you are the DPO. Resupro is in the business of helping companies in their hiring. Basically, it allows job seekers to submit their CVs, which Resupro then automatically converts into the preferred formats of the hiring companies. You know that Resupro has already put in place the usual data security measures to protect personal data, such as the ones listed here.

  What you need to do now is prepare an external-facing data protection notice for your users, meaning the people who are submitting their CVs. What I'd like you to do is use the DP Notice Generator; you can see the link to the generator here. This web page is the data protection notice generator, which is provided free by the PDPC. It should not take you more than five minutes, based on the facts I have here. Use this generator to produce the external-facing data protection notice for Resupro. I have a sample here, so we can see later to what extent you match it. So take four or five minutes to try out the generator based on these facts. After you have filled in everything, you should be able at the end to download the notice as a Word document.

  好，以上是关于问责义务的一般原则。现在我们来看这个例子。这里有两个问题：一个是对外的，一个是对内的。

  这里你是 Levin，一家初创公司 Resupro 的联合创始人。公司有五名员工，你是DPO。Resupro 的业务是帮助企业招聘。基本上，它让求职者提交简历，然后 Resupro 自动把简历转换成招聘公司偏好的格式。你知道 Resupro 已经采取了常见的数据安全措施来保护个人数据，比如这里列出的这些。

  你现在需要为用户，也就是提交简历的人，准备一份对外的数据保护通知。我希望你们使用 DP Notice Generator，链接在这里。这个网页就是数据保护通知生成器，由PDPC免费提供。根据我这里给的事实，应该不超过五分钟。用这个生成器为 Resupro 生成对外的数据保护通知。我这里有一份范本，待会儿我们可以看看你们的结果与它吻合到什么程度。所以花四五分钟，根据这些事实试试这个生成器。全部填写完后，最后你应该能把通知下载成Word文档。

- [20:15:08–20:17:51] **Walking Through the Generated Data Protection Notice**

  Has everyone managed to get through the online form? If not, we can go through the data protection notice and look at some key points.

  You should have generated a Word document like this. The first two pages are not really part of the notice, so go down to here. You can see the usual language of a data protection notice: this is how we collect, use, and disclose personal data.

  This part is basically the information you're most likely to collect from the job applicants: name, email address, telephone number, nationality, employment information, education information. The usual things you put in your CV, and you may include other things.

  Going down to here, these are the purposes for collecting, using, or disclosing the personal data, which should probably include these: performing obligations in providing services to you, complying with relevant laws, and transmitting to third parties, meaning the companies you are sending the CVs to.

  Then here you have the various timelines. Ten business days is what I used for withdrawing consent, as well as for providing access and correction. When it comes to protection of personal data, I included some of the security measures here, which were just mentioned. This indicates what measures you have taken to secure personal data.

  Below that, you'll see the very standard, non-changing sort of clauses, like accuracy, retention, transfer limitation, and so on. Then here you have the contact details of the data protection officer, which is you. That's it. So that's your typical data protection notice. You can of course use AI to generate this as well, but this is just one tool you can use, which has been, I guess, kind of sanctioned by the PDPC.

  大家都填完线上表格了吗？如果没有，我们可以一起看看这份数据保护通知，看几个要点。

  你们应该生成了这样一份Word文档。前两页其实不属于通知本身，所以往下看到这里。可以看到数据保护通知的惯常措辞：这是我们收集、使用和披露个人数据的方式。

  这部分基本上是你最可能从求职者那里收集的信息：姓名、电邮地址、电话号码、国籍、工作信息、教育信息。就是你简历里常写的那些东西，你也可以加入其他内容。

  再往下，这些是收集、使用或披露个人数据的目的，大概应包括这些：履行为你提供服务的义务、遵守相关法律，以及传送给第三方，也就是你要发送简历的那些公司。

  然后这里是各种时限。撤回同意，以及提供查阅和更正，我用的都是十个工作日。关于个人数据的保护，我把刚才提到的一些安全措施写进去了。这说明你为保护个人数据采取了哪些措施。

  再往下，你会看到非常标准、基本不变的条款，比如准确性、保留、转移限制等等。然后这里是数据保护官的联系方式，也就是你。就这样。这就是一份典型的数据保护通知。当然你也可以用AI来生成，但这只是你可以用的一个工具，而且我想它算是得到了PDPC的某种认可。

- [20:17:51–20:19:48] **Exercise: Outlining an Internal Policy for Bobby's Testimonial Task**

  So that's the notice. As I said, that's an example of the external-facing notice, and that's what we normally put on the website. The more important one is probably the internal policies you have. So let's move on to the next part of the question.

  We assume the same facts as the previous slide, but now you also know that Resupro is intending to start reaching out to job applicants who have already successfully found a job through its services, to ask them for testimonials. Basically, you want success stories, to show that your service is great. To this end, Resupro has already included an optional checkbox on its website allowing job applicants to consent to Resupro connecting with them for the purpose of showcasing success stories. And you know that many of the job seekers have checked that checkbox, because they want to be showcased.

  Bobby is the employee you have put in charge of marketing. His job is to identify these success stories and reach out to them to request a video interview, which will then be recorded and edited for publication on the website. You have already prepared a consent form for this purpose, and in the consent form you committed to only use the recording for the purpose of showcasing success stories.

  So you are now asked to outline the internal-facing policy you would need to draft so that Bobby can perform his task in compliance with the PDPA. Take a few minutes to think about how you would instruct Bobby to do this, in a way that's compliant with the PDPA, knowing what you know about all the data protection obligations.

  以上就是这份通知。正如我说的，这是对外通知的一个例子，通常就放在网站上。更重要的大概是你的内部政策。我们进入这道题的下一部分。

  假设事实与上一张幻灯片相同，但现在你还知道，Resupro 打算开始联系已经通过其服务成功找到工作的求职者，请他们提供推荐感言。基本上，你想要成功故事，来展示你的服务很好。为此，Resupro 已经在网站上加了一个可选的勾选框，让求职者同意 Resupro 为展示成功故事而与他们联系。你也知道，很多求职者都勾了这个框，因为他们想被展示。

  Bobby 是你安排负责市场营销的员工。他的工作是找出这些成功故事，联系当事人请求做一次视频访谈，访谈会被录制、剪辑后发布在网站上。你已经为此准备了一份同意书，并在同意书中承诺录像只用于展示成功故事。

  现在要求你概述需要起草的内部政策，使 Bobby 能够在遵守PDPA的前提下完成任务。花几分钟想一想，根据你对各项数据保护义务的了解，你会如何以符合PDPA的方式指导 Bobby 做这件事。

- [20:24:12–20:25:38] **Discussion: Starting the Internal Policy for Bobby — Consent to Be Contacted**

  Alright, let's come back to this question. Again, the question is: how would you draft your internal policy for Bobby, so that he can perform his task in compliance with the PDPA? Does anyone want to talk about what might be needed here?

  A student says there are quite a few things involved, but suggests starting with the purpose: why Bobby needs to reach out, and checking consent as to whether the individuals want to be reached out to. After that, there is also the consent for the interview itself.

  Yes. So I guess one of the steps you would need to include in your internal policy relates to how Bobby is going to contact a potential success story to showcase. Probably the compliance step is to check that the person he is going to contact actually did check the checkbox, which means they gave consent to being contacted. Any other steps we need?

  好，我们回到这道题。题目还是：你会如何为 Bobby 起草内部政策，使他能够在遵守PDPA的前提下完成任务？有没有人想谈谈这里可能需要什么？

  一位同学说，这里涉及不少事情，但建议先从目的入手：Bobby 为什么要联系对方，以及核查对方是否同意被联系。之后，还有访谈本身的同意。

  对。所以我想，内部政策中需要包含的一个步骤，涉及 Bobby 将如何联系一个可以展示的潜在成功案例。合规步骤大概就是：核查他要联系的人确实勾选了那个勾选框，也就是对方同意被联系。还需要哪些步骤？

- [20:25:47–20:27:25] **Discussion: Training Bobby and Asking Only Relevant Questions**

  A student says Bobby should be told the purpose of the policy and why he needs to follow it.

  Right. So I guess that's a separate thing, but you of course need to communicate the policy to Bobby, or train him on it, after you draft it.

  Another student says they would personally educate Bobby that he shouldn't be asking interviewees for unnecessary information, and certainly not sensitive personal information, while interviewing them, and that if there is a complaint or something like that, he should be prepared for it.

  Right, so in terms of the interview — sorry, I should give you some space. During the interview, of course, Bobby should try to only ask relevant questions, questions that are relevant for showcasing the success story, and not collect data that is not relevant.

  一位同学说，应该告诉 Bobby 这项政策的目的，以及他为什么需要遵守。

  对。我想这是另一回事，但你起草好政策之后，当然需要把政策传达给 Bobby，或就此对他进行培训。

  另一位同学说，他个人会教育 Bobby，在访谈时不应该向受访者索取不必要的信息，更不应该索取敏感个人信息；如果有投诉之类的情况，他也应该有所准备。

  对，就访谈而言——抱歉，我该给你们留点空间。在访谈中，Bobby 当然应该只问相关的问题，也就是与展示成功故事相关的问题，不收集无关的数据。

- [20:27:33–20:28:34] **Discussion: Outreach and the Interview Consent Form**

  A student suggests there should be pre-interview conversations, with the policy telling Bobby how to do the outreach. After working out who checked the box and who didn't, Bobby reaches out to those who checked it, and is clear in that communication about the purpose of reaching out to them and what will likely be done with their information.

  Yes. And importantly, Bobby needs to get them to sign the consent form before conducting the interview. The consent to be contacted is one thing, which is done through the checkbox. Consent to actually go through the interview is another, and that's what the consent form is for. So if the interviewee agrees to be interviewed, they should sign the consent form before you actually conduct the interview.

  一位同学建议应有访谈前的沟通，由政策告诉 Bobby 如何进行联络。先弄清楚谁勾选了、谁没有勾选，然后 Bobby 只联系勾选了的人，并在沟通中清楚说明联系他们的目的，以及他们的信息可能会被如何使用。

  对。而且重要的是，Bobby 需要在进行访谈之前让他们签署同意书。同意被联系是一回事，这是通过勾选框完成的。同意真正接受访谈是另一回事，这就是同意书的作用。所以如果受访者同意接受访谈，他们应该在你真正进行访谈之前签署同意书。

- [20:28:51–20:30:06] **Discussion: Withdrawal of Consent and Access Requests**

  A student says there should also be procedures allowing people to withdraw their consent. Once they have given consent, and even after the video recording is done, there has to be a process ensuring there is a way for them to withdraw.

  Okay, so right at the end, there should be some process in the event that the individual wants to withdraw consent.

  Another student adds that the organisation should be able to handle access requests appropriately, through the same kind of process. If someone requests their information, they should get it, so things need to be set up so that Bobby is ready to provide it. The student adds that this should be prepared before any withdrawal or access request comes in, with the information packaged so it can be provided.

  Right. I think that would probably be something the DPO, for example, would handle, since you would normally be the person who deals with these access requests. Actually, the withdrawal requests would probably also go through the DPO, who then gives instructions to Bobby to do what's necessary to comply.

  一位同学说，还应该有允许个人撤回同意的程序。一旦他们给出同意，甚至在视频录制完成之后，也必须有程序确保他们有办法撤回。

  好，所以在最后，应该有某种程序，以应对个人想撤回同意的情况。

  另一位同学补充说，机构应该能够通过同样的程序妥善处理查阅请求。如果有人要求获取自己的信息，就应该能得到，所以需要事先安排好，让 Bobby 随时可以提供。这位同学还说，应该在任何撤回或查阅请求到来之前就做好准备，把信息整理好以便提供。

  对。我认为这大概是比如DPO会处理的事情，因为通常是由你来处理这些查阅请求。其实撤回请求大概也会经由DPO，再由DPO指示 Bobby 采取必要的合规措施。

- [20:30:15–20:31:15] **Discussion: Securing the Footage and Limiting Its Use**

  A student suggests the policy should cover how Bobby secures the data and handles the personal information.

  Okay, so post-interview. Post-interview, I suppose there should be — and you don't really have a lot of instructions here — but basically, the policy should tell Bobby how the footage is to be stored.

  Another student says that, for the usage of the data after it is collected, Bobby should only use it for the testimonial purpose itself; other things like marketing outreach are not okay.

  Yes, so you should restrict the scope of the usage, and that would probably be part of the storage instructions as well. You should only use it for that one purpose.

  一位同学建议，政策应涵盖 Bobby 如何保护数据、如何处理个人信息。

  好，那就是访谈之后。访谈之后，我想应该有——你们这里给的指示不多——但基本上政策应该告诉 Bobby 录像应如何存储。

  另一位同学说，数据收集之后，Bobby 只应将其用于推荐感言这一目的，其他用途比如营销推广都不可以。

  对，所以你应该限制使用范围，这大概也会是存储指示的一部分。你只应该把它用于那一个目的。

- [20:31:24–20:32:47] **Discussion: Access Controls, Third Parties, and Retention**

  A student asks whether the policy should say that Bobby can only access certain parts of the applicants' information that he needs, for example only the phone number, and not every detail.

  Got it. That would not be part of this internal policy, which is specific to this activity. But there will also be, I suppose, organisation-wide rules regarding who can access what and who can't.

  Another student says they would like to see retention covered, as well as any third-party service providers you would engage.

  Okay, right. In this scenario, we assume there's no intermediary. But if there were, then something would have to be said about how you should deal with that. But definitely, there needs to be something about retention. In general, because this is a single-use situation, the raw footage should probably be deleted. After you have created the edited version and put it on the website, there's no need to keep the raw footage anymore, so it should be deleted as part of the retention limitation.

  一位同学问，政策是否应规定 Bobby 只能查阅他所需要的申请人部分信息，比如只看电话号码，而不是所有细节。

  明白。这不会是这份内部政策的一部分，因为这份政策是针对这项具体活动的。但我想机构层面也会有规则，规定谁可以查阅什么、谁不可以。

  另一位同学说，希望看到政策涵盖数据保留，以及你会聘用的任何第三方服务商。

  好。在这个情景中，我们假设没有数据中介。但如果有的话，就需要对如何处理这一点作出规定。不过肯定需要有关于保留的内容。总的来说，由于这是一个单一用途的情况，原始录像大概应该删除。在你制作好剪辑版本并放上网站之后，就没有必要再保留原始录像了，所以应该按照保留限制的要求把它删除。

- [20:32:56–20:34:26] **Discussion: Where Data Breach Reporting Belongs**

  A student suggests adding reporting items: if a data breach happens, Bobby should immediately notify the DPO.

  I think that would also not be part of this, because, as I said, this internal policy is specific to this particular activity. Data breach notification is something that applies across the board; it's what we would need to do in this situation and in any other situation. So I don't think you would necessarily fit that into your specific policy.

  The student follows up: but what if it's Bobby himself who mishandles the data, for example sending it to the wrong person? He should still follow the procedure.

  So you could put that in the internal policy related to this specific activity. Or there could be general employee instructions that apply to all employees, which basically say that whenever there's any data breach you're involved in, you need to report it. So I guess it's a choice where you want to put this.

  一位同学建议加入报告事项：如果发生数据泄露，Bobby 应立即通知DPO。

  我认为这也不会是这份政策的一部分，因为正如我所说，这份内部政策是针对这项具体活动的。数据泄露通知是普遍适用的，无论在这个情况还是任何其他情况下我们都需要这样做。所以我认为不一定要把它放进这份专门的政策里。

  这位同学追问：如果是 Bobby 自己处理数据出错，比如发给了错误的人呢？他仍然应该遵循程序。

  你可以把这一点放进与这项具体活动相关的内部政策中，也可以有适用于全体员工的一般员工指示，基本上规定：只要你涉及任何数据泄露，就需要报告。所以我想这是一个选择，看你想把它放在哪里。

- [20:34:26–20:35:52] **Discussion: Telling Interviewees About Edits; Reviewing the Draft**

  A student says Bobby should also make sure the interviewee knows what will be done with the footage and what kinds of edits will be made to it. For example, if the interviewee uses a poor choice of words, Bobby probably needs to make sure the final version is appropriate.

  Sure, although I think that would be dealt with within the consent form itself, because the consent form already tells the interviewee what the whole interview is for. So I guess that comes within the consent form, but it makes it important that Bobby ensures the consent form is signed.

  The student adds that after the interview, Bobby may want to prepare a draft and show it to the interviewee for approval.

  Yes, so I think that would be part of the more practical steps Bobby would want to take. It's not necessarily about compliance, but about the actual step-by-step process Bobby would be undertaking when doing this particular task.

  一位同学说，Bobby 还应确保受访者知道录像会被如何使用、会做哪些剪辑。比如，如果受访者用词不当，Bobby 大概需要确保最终版本是恰当的。

  当然，不过我认为这会在同意书里处理，因为同意书已经告诉受访者整个访谈是做什么用的。所以我想这属于同意书的范围，但这也说明 Bobby 确保同意书被签署是很重要的。

  这位同学补充说，访谈之后，Bobby 也许想先做一个草稿给受访者看，征得同意。

  对，我认为这会是 Bobby 想采取的更实际的步骤之一。它不一定关乎合规，而是关乎 Bobby 在执行这项任务时实际的逐步流程。

- [20:35:52–20:37:11] **Discussion: Escalating to the DPO and Preparing an FAQ**

  A student suggests the policy should include an escalation path: if there's a data breach, or in any particular case where Bobby has to deal with something, he should always go to the DPO. It could be a data breach, or a complaint from the interviewee that they don't want something specific included. Bobby needs to be able to report it.

  Yeah, okay. I guess you could include something along the lines of: when in doubt, just let the DPO know if there's any data protection issue.

  The student agrees, whether it is a breach or a withdrawal of consent.

  Another student, relating to that, says there will be common questions that interviewees or potential interviewees will have. So instead of Bobby answering on the spot, the policy could attach an FAQ on common questions to the prepared form, and only if a question isn't covered there would it be escalated to the DPO. Otherwise the DPO will be really busy.

  Sure. So you can have a sort of FAQ on what to do if certain things happen, and if in doubt, report to the DPO, which may be less work for you later on.

  一位同学建议政策应包含上报途径：如果发生数据泄露，或在任何 Bobby 需要处理某件事的特定情况下，他都应该找DPO。可能是数据泄露，也可能是受访者投诉说不希望某些具体内容被放进去。Bobby 需要能够上报。

  好的。我想你可以加入类似这样的内容：如有疑问，只要有任何数据保护问题，就告知DPO。

  这位同学表示同意，无论是泄露还是撤回同意。

  另一位同学接着说，受访者或潜在受访者会有一些常见问题。所以与其让 Bobby 当场回答，政策可以在准备好的表格后附上常见问题解答（FAQ），只有当问题不在其中时才上报给DPO。否则DPO会非常忙。

  当然。所以你可以准备一份类似FAQ的东西，说明发生某些情况时该怎么做；如有疑问，再上报DPO，这样你之后的工作可能会少一些。

- [20:37:22–20:38:32] **Discussion: Researching Candidates Before Contact**

  One more thing you would want to deal with is that even before the contact stage, Bobby would be doing research on who is a likely candidate. Here again, you want to make sure that if he is looking at non-public data, he should be doing so only for individuals who checked the checkbox. Or he can rely on public information, like company websites and so on, to figure out who was successful.

  A student adds that the instructions should limit Bobby to using only the information he actually needs.

  Sure. So this would be part of the idea that you should only be asking relevant questions, and only collecting information relevant to creating the success story.

  还有一件你需要处理的事：甚至在联系阶段之前，Bobby 就会研究谁可能是合适的人选。同样，你要确保如果他查看的是非公开数据，那么只能针对勾选了勾选框的个人。或者他可以依靠公开信息，比如公司网站等，来找出谁成功找到了工作。

  一位同学补充说，指示应限制 Bobby 只使用他真正需要的信息。

  当然。这属于"只问相关问题、只收集与制作成功故事相关的信息"这一思路的一部分。

- [20:38:45–20:40:15] **Wrap-Up: Internal vs External Policies; Next Topic**

  Okay, so there are some choices you can make, but this is what a fairly straightforward internal policy would look like. You consider step by step what the person has to do, figure out whether there are data protection issues at each step, and then decide what you should do to be compliant. Sometimes this is built into the instructions given to Bobby; sometimes it is separate. Any questions about this?

  So I hope this makes it clear that external policies and internal policies are two very important things. The internal one is a highly practical document. The external one is more like a brochure, to show the public what we do in very general terms.

  If there are no questions, that's all I've got for today's lesson. We have finally done all the data protection obligations per se. For the next few lessons, we'll be looking at things outside of the obligations. In the following lessons, we are going to look at enforcement, which is how the PDPC and/or individuals can potentially enforce the PDPA against organisations, and, more to the point, how organisations can respond.

  好，这里有一些可以选择的地方，但一份相当直接的内部政策大致就是这样。你要一步一步考虑这个人需要做什么，找出每一步是否存在数据保护问题，然后决定应该怎么做才能合规。有时这会直接融入给 Bobby 的指示中，有时则是独立的。对此有什么问题吗？

  我希望这让大家清楚，对外政策和内部政策是两样非常重要的东西。内部政策是一份高度实用的文件。对外政策更像是一本宣传册，用非常笼统的方式向公众展示我们做什么。

  如果没有问题，今天的课就到这里。我们终于讲完了所有的数据保护义务本身。接下来几节课，我们会看义务之外的内容。在之后的课上，我们将讨论执法（enforcement），也就是PDPC和/或个人可以如何对机构执行PDPA，更重要的是，机构可以如何应对。
